Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the reactions space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 975 and the convergence rate is O(534/n^2).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_975(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 975. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^534(S, F) is trivial. QED.
